UPDATE "PMS_RPT_FINDINGS" set 
"NAME"=t.LOCAL_DISP_NAME ,
"OMITTED_NAME"=t.LOCAL_DISP_NAME ,
"CHOOSE_NAME"=t.LOCAL_DISP_NAME ,
"DISP_NAME"=t.LOCAL_DISP_NAME ,
"SINGULAR"=case when "SINGULAR" ="NAME" then t.LOCAL_DISP_NAME else "SINGULAR" end
from TEMP_PMS_RPT_FINDINGS t where "PMS_RPT_FINDINGS"."RPT_FINDING_ID"=t.RPT_FINDING_ID
